# Batch 1: manual contact-partner review

Proposals for independent review, not publication. Reviewed all 17 stable targets and all 634 raw observations from contacts-a263bf5f433dba68c0c4. Evidence is bounded to existing partner/PDB records: 341 primary PDB entries and 1,126 polymer-entity records cached. Rules preserve raw labels and source observations. The ledger records each exact partner/PDB/context group, its observation IDs, disposition, primary links and construct caveats. No shared repository, database or deployment was changed.

There are 199 proposed rules. Every rule matches at least one input observation using the production matcher, with no effective conflicts. All references are single clickable URLs; additional sources are in reasons/this report. Scoped rules override broad role rules. Therapeutic matched arms/components remain named with provenance; none is excluded solely because the whole drug was not crystallized. Receptor/assembly variants retain receptor_partner category; labels preserve distinct constructs. Unknown variant origin remains explicitly uncertain, not silently native.

## Counts and review coverage

Counts are estimated unique named identities using current naming/EC filters after applying the rules. They may increase when previously bare accessions become identified or engineered variants are separated. They are not counts of independent experiments, exhaustive binders, or validated cellular accessibility. Parent rebuild is authoritative.

## Per-target findings

### GABRG2 — P18507

Reviewed the complete channel-subunit series. GABRA/GABRB identities are receptor assembly components, not soluble agonists. Puerarin has human clinical-study evidence. Megabody38 is the same reagent across B5Z8H1 scaffold and SAbDab labels at9FAJ. Deposited bovine/human GABRA1 chimeras and GABRB3–cytochrome b562 fusions require explicit construct labels; a bare bovine P08219 record remains unclassified where no human chimera mapping is established. NLGN2 in9FAJ is a33-residue transmembrane/cytoplasmic segment, and LHFPL4 membrane contacts remain all-scope only. No inference of native extracellular accessibility from these assembly contacts.

### NCSTN — Q92542

PSEN1/2,PEN2,APH1A/B are gamma-secretase assembly components. APP/NOTCH1 observations are substrate fragments. Sequence comparison identifies APP precursor V695C at6IYC,K699C at8X52,I702C at8X54 despite zero mutation counts in latter entries; these remain distinct engineered ligands. PSEN1 variants remain receptor_partner because category describes assembly role.8OQZ entity5 is deposited Amyloid-beta1–46, not an antibody called Ab46; its sequence is allX, so nomenclature is depositor evidence rather than sequence validation. The study/title and deposited precursor lengths need not match the modeled processed substrate length.

### GRIN1 — Q05586

Reviewed every deposited GRIN2/GRIN3 partner/PDB group. These are channel assembly partners. Canonical sequence comparisons distinguish GRIN2 variants and normalize9D3C contradictory S588C annotation to deposited C588S sequence. Q00959 is rat glutamate-receptor material and remains unclassified without human-equivalence promotion.8ZH7 is patient-derived antibody003-102, treated as a research Fab construct, not the therapeutic blocking antibody implied by the paper title. P0DP23 calmodulin contacts are cytoplasmic and preserved; no EC exclusion rule is needed.

### CALCRL — Q16602

PAMP-20 source aliases are wrong for deposited mature adrenomedullin. Native matureAM,AM25–52 fragment,and two engineeredAM37–52 variants are kept distinct.5V6Y uses four sequence substitutions plus terminal chemistry (mutation counter5);6V2E four substitutions. These ECD structures use MBP-RAMP-CLR fusion constructs. Alpha-CGRP aliases collapse only within verified structures; ADM2/intermedin is distinct. Olcegepant has randomized clinical evidence. Erenumab remains named binding-arm evidence. Intracellular GNB1 and membrane-spanning engineered GNAS remain source observations, with no claim of extracellular ligand status.

### CD74 — P04233

Canonical HLA-DRA/DRB1/DQA1/DQB1 are assembly partners.3QXD HLA-DRAF54C is qualified. A0A1V1IGJ9,A0A4E9DJJ3,D7RIG5 are HLA beta-chain records; allele equivalence to canonicalDRB1 is not invented. P14434/P14483 are mouseMHCII chains, not human endogenous partners.1ICF is the invariant-chain thyroglobulin domain inhibiting cathepsinL, an endolysosomal luminal complex: preserve all scope, propose EC-overview exclusion only. Surface exposure is not established by luminal-domain topology.

### ITGAM — P11215

107 Fab and IEDB107 aliases collapse, including the assay withoutPDB. hCD11bNb1 is distinct. C3a source labels instead describe C3dC1010A at4M76,iC3b at7AKK,and a C3dg–nanobody fusion at9T3Y.4M76 mixed mutation field includes receptor mutations, which are not assigned toC3. Pleiotrophin N- and C-terminal domains at8VOH/8VOI remain one ligand identity with domain-specific evidence. SIM is simvastatin without implied integrin indication. P0DKX7 is Bordetella toxin fragment;6RHWQ2FWN9/Q2FWP0 are Staphylococcal cytolysin components. Their raw rows remain unclassified, not human endogenous.9T5V/9T5Z ITGB2 mutation counter3 conflicts with no aligned canonical substitutions; no unsupported distinct ligand is invented solely from that counter.

### IL2RG — P31785

REGN7257 clinical Fab and REGN9432 research Fab are two different antibodies in8EPA. Gamma-c-VHH6 is a third binder. NativeIL2,IL2C125A,D10,and the clinical IL2v cytokine component are distinct.3QAZ deposited D10 sequence has matureR81D, despite Addgene textR81F; use the deposited sequence.5M5E has matureT3A/F42A/Y45A/L72G/C125A despite counter0; cytokine-component evidence does not imply entire cergutuzumab amunaleukin coordinates. NativeIL4 differs from RGA eight-substitution variant. IL21N68Q is engineered. Receptor glycosylation variants retain receptor_partner role and canonical precursor numbering; e.g IL2RBN29Q/N43Q/N71Q corresponds to matureN3Q/N17Q/N45Q.

### TMPRSS2 — O15393

T22,VHH77,VHH77-10,andA07 are distinct research binders.8V04GBS is nafamostat-derived guanidinobenzoyl acyl moiety, not intact nafamostat. M9ZTT7 is TcsH toxin;8JI0 MBP mapping is a fusion component. HKU1 strain-specific spike and NL63S2-prime substrate observations remain unclassified and distinct, including9OPQ composite NL63-S2prime/HKU1-RBD. Some deposited source-organism fields incorrectly say Candidatus Accumulibacter for spike entities; this contradiction is recorded, not promoted into a bacterial identity. All raw viral/toxin observations retained.

### ITGAL — P20701

Efalizumab structural/therapeutic aliases merge within3EOA/3EOB. The no-PDB IEDB label combines parentalMHM24 and humanizedefalizumab; leave that mixed identity unresolved. AL57 remains distinct. BMS587101/2IC has clinical investigation. NativeICAM1 differs from engineered3TCXD1: primary methods give Q1M/T2V/I10T/T23A/P38V/P63V/S67A/T78A. ICAM3 andICAM5 are separate adhesion ligands. ITGB2 mutation names are reconciled against sequence rather than blindly usingN232R depositor field.

### PTCH1 — Q13635

NB64 andNB75 are distinct research nanobodies; each source-alias group collapses. SHH is the processed signaling-domain ligand, with lipidation/truncation context.6OEV title and PLM residue establish native palmitoylation; count1 must not be read automatically as a substitution. Y01 is synthetic cholesteryl hemisuccinate, not native cholesterol.

### IL2RB — P14784

Reciprocal cytokine identities and variants match IL2RG review. Beta-chainVHH6 is distinct from gamma-chainVHH6. IL2RG matureN53Q/depositionN58Q/precursorN75Q labels refer to the same canonicalN75Q substitution and use one name.5M5ECYS remains raw unclassified: chemical identity alone does not establish free endogenous ligand rather than additive/adduct. D3HJY4 at6E8K is bacterial LeSH binding intracellular IL2RB material; retained in all scope, not renamed human JAK or endogenous ligand.

### CD81 — P60033

Six antibody identities remain:CD81K13,CD81K04,scFv4,scFv5,scFv10,5A6. Only same-clone aliases merge; overlapping footprints do not establish equivalence. Cholesterol5TCX is endogenous small molecule in membrane cavity, retained in all scope. CD19 is an assembly partner with membrane-spanning contact context.

### CSF2RB — P32927

Trabikibart=CSL311 binding arm, including heavy/light constant-region accession rows for the deposited sameFab. BION1 remains distinct. GMCSF andIL5 cytokines are native sequences; IL3 at6NMY is matureW13Y=precursorW32Y, consistent with preceding IL3RA review. IL3RA N194Q remains qualified receptor_partner.4NKQ annotationN346Q appears onCSF2RAentity although canonical alignment is unchanged and beta-receptor entity reports a difference: do not assign that mutation to the wrong protein.

### NRP1 — O14786

Vesencumab/YW107.4.87/anti-Nrp1B and matched-arm source aliases merge at2QQN. EG00229 andEG01377 are distinct research compounds.6TKK VEGFB accession is an acetylated synthetic C-terminal hexapeptide, not intact nativeVEGFB.9EOU peptide VDTYDGGISVVYGLR matches primarypatentFOL026 and differs from nativeosteopontin andFOL005; tool category, no inferred clinical status. VGF-derived peptide records retain fragment qualifiers. Chlorotoxin8C5G is a known foreignscorpion peptide, keptunclassified. SARS-CoV2 peptide7JJC remains sourceaccession/unclassified; no invented intactspike binding claim.

### NOTCH3 — Q9UM47

MOR20350 andMOR20358 aliases merge separately. AntiN3ratFab6XSW remains a third research reagent; no equivalence to clinicalPF06650808 is inferred from the preclinicalADC paper. P31266 is mouseRBPJ intracellular assembly material; preservedunclassified with compartmentcontext.

### TGFA — P01135

LY3016859=Fepixnebart matched Fab; clinical primary source supports therapeutic category. tAb2 separate research antibody. EGFR is receptor partner.3E50 is IDEE111Q substrate-trapping enzyme with mature soluble TGFalpha 40–89: tool construct retained in both scopes; this does not show binderaccess to membrane-tethered precursor.

### EPHA4 — P54764

EFNB2,EFNB3,EFNA5 are separate ephrin ligands.2WO3 EFNA2 is N174Q, confirmed by UniProt alignment and primarymethods; keep engineeredvariant.7OFV is synthetic six-residue modified peptidomimetic; no guessed compound-number name. ReceptorC204A is not a ligandmutation.

## Evidence limitations and unresolved decisions

Sequence checks are local alignments of deposited canonical entity sequences to current UniProt canonical sequences. They identify represented substitutions; they do not prove a complete drug sequence, native folding, chain-contact ownership, or functional effect. Signal peptides, tags, deletions and modified monomers require separate interpretation. `sequence-checks.json` and `variant-sequence-checks.json` contain alignments. Mutation-count zero is not proof of wild type; conversely terminal amidation/palmitoylation can inflate mutation counters.

No-PDB mixed MHM24/efalizumab IEDB identity remains unresolved. CYS5M5E remains raw unclassified pending chemical-role/covalent-link review. Foreign/MHC/viral records retain their original identity unless unambiguous deposited nomenclature supports a conservative name; no human-equivalence or physiological category is invented. ITGB2 9T5V/9T5Z mutation-count-only discrepancies do not create new named variants. GNAS/GNB1/calmodulin/LeSH/RBPJ cytoplasmic evidence is retained. The EC-only exclusion proposed for CD74–CTSL 1ICF is supported by endolysosomal localization. TGFA–IDE E111Q 3E50 remains in both scopes: soluble-fragment context alone does not establish non-EC localization.

Matcher caveats: matching strips terminal Fab/Fv/VHH and parenthetical source suffixes. Exact original labels were checked. No rule uses raw numeric IEDB IDs. Ambiguous generic peptide rules are PDB-scoped. Broad rules are limited to stable target/accession or a named antibody clone; structure-specific construct corrections take precedence.

39. **P18507 / 9faj / Megabody38** (tool). Original names: `B5Z8H1`; `sabdab2_H01PPL0000`; `sabdab2_H01PPL0000 (SD-H)`. Entity 6 is Megabody38. B5Z8H1 is its scaffold mapping, not an independent human partner. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/9FAJ).

40. **Q05586 / 8zh7 / Antibody 003-102** (tool). Original names: `sabdab2_H059DL0413`; `sabdab2_H059DL0413 (FAB)`. Entities 2/3 identify this antibody; no inferred clinical identity. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/8ZH7).

41. **Q92542 / 8oqz / Amyloid-beta 1–46 substrate fragment** (endogenous_large). Original names: `peptide`. Entity 5 is explicitly amyloid-beta 1–46. Deposited sequence is unresolved X characters; name comes from depositor, not sequence confirmation; not an antibody called Ab46. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/8OQZ).

42. **Q92542 / 6iyc / APP V695C substrate construct** (tool). Original names: `P05067`; `APP`. Deposited sequence alignment to canonical APP P05067 verifies indicated precursor substitution (respectively AβV24C/K28C/I31C). 8X52/8X54 mutation-count zero conflicts with sequence; title describes substrate processing state, not entire deposited entity. Additional primary sources: https://www.uniprot.org/uniprotkb/P05067/entry [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6IYC).

43. **Q92542 / 8x52 / APP-derived Aβ49 K699C construct** (tool). Original names: `P05067`; `APP`. Deposited sequence alignment to canonical APP P05067 verifies indicated precursor substitution (respectively AβV24C/K28C/I31C). 8X52/8X54 mutation-count zero conflicts with sequence; title describes substrate processing state, not entire deposited entity. Additional primary sources: https://www.uniprot.org/uniprotkb/P05067/entry [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/8X52).

44. **Q92542 / 8x54 / APP-C99 I702C construct** (tool). Original names: `P05067`; `APP`. Deposited sequence alignment to canonical APP P05067 verifies indicated precursor substitution (respectively AβV24C/K28C/I31C). 8X52/8X54 mutation-count zero conflicts with sequence; title describes substrate processing state, not entire deposited entity. Additional primary sources: https://www.uniprot.org/uniprotkb/P05067/entry [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/8X54).

45. **Q92542 / 6idf / NOTCH1 substrate fragment** (endogenous_large). Original names: `P46531`; `NOTCH1`. Deposited substrate fragment within gamma-secretase assembly; not intact surface protein accessibility. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6IDF).

46. **Q16602 / 3n7s / Olcegepant** (therapeutic). Original names: `3N6`. CCD identity and randomized human clinical study establish investigational therapeutic. Additional primary sources: https://doi.org/10.1056/NEJMoa030505 [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/ligand/3N6).

47. **Q16602 / 6umg / Erenumab** (therapeutic). Original names: `Erenumab`; `sabdab2_H0204L01M1`; `sabdab2_H0204L01M1 (FAB)`. Deposited Fab and therapeutic matched-arm record refer to erenumab binding arm; not whole-IgG coordinates. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6UMG).

48. **Q16602 / 7knu / α-CGRP** (endogenous_large). Original names: `P06881`; `α-CGRP`; `Calcitonin gene-related peptide 1`. Entity and precursor accession identify alpha-CGRP mature peptide, not calcitonin. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/7KNU).

49. **Q16602 / 6e3y / α-CGRP** (endogenous_large). Original names: `P06881`; `α-CGRP`; `Calcitonin gene-related peptide 1`. Entity and precursor accession identify alpha-CGRP mature peptide, not calcitonin. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6E3Y).

50. **Q16602 / 6uun / Adrenomedullin** (endogenous_large). Original names: `P35318`; `PAMP-20`; `Adrenomedullin`. Deposited mature adrenomedullin; PAMP-20 is a different peptide from same precursor and is erroneous here. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6UUN).

51. **Q16602 / 6uus / Adrenomedullin** (endogenous_large). Original names: `P35318`; `PAMP-20`; `Adrenomedullin`. Deposited mature adrenomedullin; PAMP-20 is a different peptide from same precursor and is erroneous here. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6UUS).

52. **Q16602 / 4rwf / Adrenomedullin 25–52 fragment** (endogenous_large). Original names: `P35318`; `PAMP-20`. Entity 2 sequence KLAHQIYQFTDKDKDNVAPRSKISPQGY plus terminal modification denotes AM25–52, not PAMP20. Target is MBP-RAMP2-CLR fusion. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/4RWF).

53. **Q16602 / 5v6y / AM(37–52) S45W/K46L/Q50W/Y52F antagonist** (tool). Original names: `P35318`; `PAMP-20`. Entity 2 DKDNVAPRWLISPWGF plus terminal X; primary paper identifies four engineered substitutions. Mutation-count 5 includes terminal chemistry and is not five biological substitutions. Additional primary sources: https://doi.org/10.1124/mol.117.110916 [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/5V6Y).

54. **Q16602 / 6v2e / Adrenomedullin 37–52 S45R/K46L/S48G/Q50W variant** (tool). Original names: `P35318`; `PAMP-20`. Entry title and entity 2 sequence DKDNVAPRRLIGPWGY establish engineered four-substitution peptide. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6V2E).

55. **Q16602 / 6d1u / Adrenomedullin 2/intermedin** (endogenous_large). Original names: `Q7Z4H4`; `adrenomedullin 2/intermedin`. Deposited ADM2/intermedin; 6D1U is C-terminal fragment (precursor129–147) in MBP-RAMP1-CLR fusion. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6D1U).

56. **Q16602 / 6uva / Adrenomedullin 2/intermedin** (endogenous_large). Original names: `Q7Z4H4`; `adrenomedullin 2/intermedin`. Deposited ADM2/intermedin; 6D1U is C-terminal fragment (precursor129–147) in MBP-RAMP1-CLR fusion. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/6UVA).

57. **P04233 / 1icf / Cathepsin L** (endogenous_large). Original names: `P07711`; `CTSL`. Native cathepsin L heavy/light chains bind invariant-chain thyroglobulin domain. Endolysosomal luminal inhibitory complex; EC residue topology does not establish cell-surface accessibility. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/1ICF).

58. **P11215 / 3q3g / 107 antibody** (tool). Original names: `107 Fab`; `107`; `sabdab2_H00KSL00HE`; `sabdab2_H00KSL00HE (FAB)`. Research antibody 107 Fab; same clone across structural/IEDB aliases. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/3Q3G).

59. **P11215 / 3qa3 / 107 antibody** (tool). Original names: `107 Fab`; `107`; `sabdab2_H00KSL00HE`; `sabdab2_H00KSL00HE (FAB)`. Research antibody 107 Fab; same clone across structural/IEDB aliases. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/3QA3).

60. **P11215 / 7p2d / hCD11bNb1** (tool). Original names: `hCD11bNb1 Nanobody`. Named research nanobody; no inferred clinical identity. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/7P2D).

61. **P11215 / 4xw2 / Simvastatin** (therapeutic). Original names: `SIM`. Deposited SIM is simvastatin; retain observed integrin interaction without implying approved integrin indication. Additional primary sources: https://www.rcsb.org/structure/4XW2 [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/ligand/SIM).

62. **P11215 / 4m76 / C3d C1010A fragment** (tool). Original names: `P01024`; `C3a (C3)`. Structure is C3d, not C3a. Entity 1 spans precursor994–1288 and has C1010A. Mixed mutation field also lists receptor C144A/I332G; these are not C3 substitutions. [Primary source](https://www.rcsb.org/structure/4M76).
